function MiniGauge({label, value, max = 100, color = "bg-amber-500", showValue = true}) {
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100));
  return m.jsxs("div", {className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 py-[0.5px] text-[0.55rem] leading-none", children: [
    m.jsx("span", {className: "w-10 shrink-0 text-stone-400 font-medium", children: label}),
    m.jsx("div", {className: "flex-1 h-1 bg-stone-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-stone-700/50", children:
      m.jsx("div", {className: `h-full ${color} rounded-full transition-all`, style: {width: `${pct}%`}})
    }),
    showValue && m.jsx("span", {className: "w-6 shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums text-stone-300 font-mono text-[0.5rem]", children: value})
  ]});
}

function MiniGauge({label, value, max = 100, color = "bg-amber-500", showValue = true}) {
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100));
  return m.jsxs("div", {className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 py-[0.5px] text-[0.55rem] leading-none", children: [
    m.jsx("span", {className: "w-10 shrink-0 text-stone-400 font-medium", children: label}),
    m.jsx("div", {className: "flex-1 h-1 bg-stone-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-stone-700/50", children:
      m.jsx("div", {className: `h-full ${color} rounded-full transition-all`, style: {width: `${pct}%`}})
    }),
    showValue && m.jsx("span", {className: "w-6 shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums text-stone-300 font-mono text-[0.5rem]", children: value})
  ]});
}

function MiniGauge({label, value, max = 100, color = "bg-amber-500", showValue = true}) {
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100));
  return m.jsxs("div", {className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 py-[0.5px] text-[0.55rem] leading-none", children: [
    m.jsx("span", {className: "w-10 shrink-0 text-stone-400 font-medium", children: label}),
    m.jsx("div", {className: "flex-1 h-1 bg-stone-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-stone-700/50", children:
      m.jsx("div", {className: `h-full ${color} rounded-full transition-all`, style: {width: `${pct}%`}})
    }),
    showValue && m.jsx("span", {className: "w-6 shrink-0 text-right tabular-nums text-stone-300 font-mono text-[0.5rem]", children: value})
  ]});
}
